# Paper statement inventory · `reg:inventory:overview`

**Setup (Scope)** · `setup:inventory:scope` The inventory contains 1751 statement rows and 2833 constant entries. The paper release is network-v0.37.0-draft.1. The inventory covers explicit labels and labels generated by structure and frontier environments at this release; the two censuses are separate. Each refresh re-reads every inventory row against the new release and rewrites current_at to that release; it records the text reflected by the row, not when the row was introduced. The constants field refreshed_at likewise identifies its projection edition. The inventory distinguishes 1717 explicit labels and 34 environment-generated labels; neither census silently substitutes for the other. The exact-label material_class records the paper’s closure, oracle or courtesy class independently of repository conformance classification; pure mathematics belongs to closure at this tag. Implementation kinds and proposed homes are engineering classifications, not claims that code exists or that a paper question is settled. The decomposition is recorded in (`rec:decomposition:decision`). A kind or home of none assigns no new implementation item or no Layer 1 home, respectively; documentation can still have an owner.

**Convention (Label presentation)** · `conv:inventory:labels` Paper labels are exhibited with doubled delimiters because the imported registry format is unavailable under the repository namespace. The citable field preserves eligibility under the adopted kind relation for a project-neutral import format; it does not claim that an import is active. Paper label kinds remain unchanged. Environment text is the inventory's recorded description, while implementation kind describes a proposed code responsibility. Short subdivision rows locate separately labelled cases, conditions and steps; their titles do not replace the normative statement or supply its premises.

Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | courtesy | invariant check | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``post:comparator:accountability-cost`` | postulate | Interpretation or optional practice, not a closure admission condition: Keep attributed immutable claims and their endogenous accountability cost; internal corroboration cannot establish external truth or supply an authenticity test. | courtesy | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``tbl:comparator:two-costs`` | table | Distinguish exogenous source cost from endogenous accountability cost. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``subsec:comparator:faithfulness`` | subsection | Track faithfulness as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``post:comparator:faithfulness`` | postulate | Under supplied source assumptions, require the dimensional, immutable-ledger, pair-conservation, epoch-set, title, memoryless, universal-act, domain, tilt, cluster-mean and final-state disciplines. Routes bypassing them are unfloored injections. Construction and certificates support the calculation clauses; final-set admission's exclusive use of the completed post-debit result remains a boundary obligation. These properties imply neither partition invariance nor a universal standing-response sign. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-source`` | condition | Assume the supplied contribution has source backing, irrecoverability and its opaque identity association. These premises preserve provenance, not standing across alternative funded partitions; identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-numeraire`` | condition | Divide each frame-denominated input by the positive numeraire before combining it with a count. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-measurement`` | condition | Compute standing as a measurement of the record, not a possessed ledger asset; the ledger after measurement must equal the ledger before it. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-conservation`` | condition | Conserve full balance and count totals through row-stochastic allocation; this is rival allocation, not invariance of individual standings, participant maxima or unweighted sums. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-epoch-set`` | condition | Keep host-assigned order, relation frontiers and intra-epoch latest positions out of every standing quantity. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-title`` | condition | Resolve a settlement act’s counterparty allocation only through the epoch title fold. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-memorylessness`` | condition | Use no previous epoch’s standing as a semantic input. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-universal-act-weighing`` | condition | Weigh every accepted Actor-authored act exactly once, resolving to a person or self; consume matched hyper-acts atomically so unmatched legs and passive artifacts carry no standing. Admissible partitions can change author-indexed folds and normalized allocation, so invariance needs additional premises. The all-state hop-intertwining premises of ``thm:dynamics:split-hop-refinement`` are a sufficient invariance criterion, not a consequence of act partition alone. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-domain-weights`` | condition | Apply declared domain weights to complete folded acts; preserving aggregate ledger pairs does not by itself preserve the resulting author-indexed allocation. The all-state hop-intertwining premises of ``thm:dynamics:split-hop-refinement`` are a sufficient invariance criterion, not a consequence of act partition alone. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-responsive-tilt`` | condition | Use recipient standing only to modulate relative authored allocation, with hop fading at the greatest certified rung. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-cluster-rate`` | condition | Preserve the closed transported-count-weighted mean under internal pair allocation; neither an open fibre mean nor individual coordinates or maxima are thereby fixed. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:faithfulness-final-set-admission`` | condition | Require final-set admission to read only the completed post-debit standing result; this is a boundary-operator obligation, not a consequence of the standing construction. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``ins:comparator:universal-amplifier`` | insight | Terminal value channels use dimensionless standing amplification without manufacturing source. Use published standing in terminal formulas only under their fixed-context premises; terminal scalar monotonicity does not imply monotonicity under a frame gross amount, act or funded partition. Partition readout bounds require the matched creators and fixed non-standing context of ``prop:transport:split-terminal-readout-bounds``. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:amplifier-rendering`` | remark | Terminal amplification must not silently become an admission-policy control. Zero standing retains unit amplification; an optional guild exponent is published with canonical default one. Terminal input monotonicity implies no monotone frame gross amount, act or partition response. Partition readout bounds require the matched creators and fixed non-standing context of ``prop:transport:split-terminal-readout-bounds``. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:conserved-allocation-scope`` | remark | Conservation excludes passive or unowned redirection and makes source allocation rival, including against self, but supplies no target veto. General funded-partition standing invariance is false even with proportional pairs and unchanged incoming acts. Pair totals, closed weighted means and equal-rate regimes survive; displacement bounds are not small strategic-gain guarantees. Concentration and invariant claims require ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` and ``thm:dynamics:split-hop-refinement``; contraction bounds residual amplification, not residual size. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cor:comparator:sybil-resistance-is-faithfulness`` | corollary | Sybil resistance means no standing without genuine contributing source and rival allocation of existing source; it promises no invariance when one participant funds several addresses. Under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``, a record-based remedy imposes the same change on a legitimate completion with identical public fields, or the same distribution of changes if randomized. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``subsec:comparator:capacity-and-admission-benchmarks`` | subsection | Track capacity and admission benchmarks as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``eq:comparator:admission-benchmark-deposits`` | equation | Source oracle condition, not a closure operand or proof of source truth: Multiply unsupported-act burn benchmarks by the realization unit-cost projection. | oracle | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``tbl:comparator:admission-benchmarks`` | table | Check debit-only, safety and door-neutral unsupported-act costs separately. | closure | fixture | pn-verification | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``subsec:comparator:the-surface`` | subsection | Track the surface as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | constant | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:network-prices-the-frame`` | remark | Derive standing from imported source without importing a realization monetary mechanism. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:surface-readings`` | remark | External prices, denominations, event costs, capacity and operating arrangements are outside the calculation; closure consumes supplied Actor entries under its explicit hypotheses. | oracle | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``subsec:comparator:limitations`` | subsection | Track limitations as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:inherited-limitations`` | remark | Closure preserves supplied amount attribution but does not make funded Actor partition neutral for standing. Aggregate export is capacity-bounded while concentration can increase a member's standing. Additional Registration debit and aggregate frame increment are different quantities. | closure | none | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:structural-ceiling`` | remark | Internal act formation and title recognition are checkable, but consumed graph data cannot establish the truth of an asserted external event. | 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:act-ownership-invariant`` | remark | An edge projection cannot independently change any parent-owned approval, payload, debit or clock. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``cor:graph:hyper-act-indivisibility`` | corollary | Both hyper-act projections share exactly one lifecycle and accounting unit. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:ontology-promotion`` | remark | Treat accepted acts as authored history and every edge as a derived projection. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:authored-act-hyper`` | case | Branch of ``def:graph:authored-act``: A hyper-edge act projects to exactly two matched records, one A-leg and one T-leg; accept neither other cardinalities nor per-leg counting or debiting. | 04 Graph | ``def:graph:raw-snapshot-path`` | definition | Use bounded simple paths in the current raw snapshot without increasing-time constraints. | closure | type | pn-readouts | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``thm:graph:raw-weight-ceiling`` | theorem | Raw canonical edge weights are nonnegative and strictly below one. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:raw-weight-ceiling-positive-walk`` | case | Branch of ``thm:graph:raw-weight-ceiling`` proof: Because every routing-live raw edge has weight below one, removing a repeated-node cycle from a positive-weight walk strictly improves its product and shortens it. This simplification is not valid for a fixed parity register. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:raw-weight-ceiling-zero-walk`` | case | Branch of ``thm:graph:raw-weight-ceiling`` proof: Removing a cycle from a zero-weight walk cannot decrease its product; repeated removal gives a simple path within the same upper depth bound when parity is unconstrained. | 04 Graph | ``def:graph:canonical-erasure-default`` | definition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Select the structural revision head before rendering. | 04 Graph | ``case:graph:passivity-source-actor`` | case | Branch of ``ax:graph:passivity``: An edge whose source is an Actor is authored by that source Actor. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:passivity-passive-terminal-leg`` | case | Branch of ``ax:graph:passivity``: A record sourced from a passive node must be the T-leg of an Actor-authored hyper-edge; no third authorship case exists. | 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:passivity-non-autonomy`` | item | Permit no passive node to initiate, redirect or modify an edge; its outgoing recorded legs are fixed by the Actor at creation and remain immutable. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``prop:graph:author-source-identity`` | proposition | The recorded author, act-identifier author, supplied frame association and binary source or hyper-edge A-leg name the same Actor. No leg has an independent author. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``lem:graph:passive-out-degree`` | lemma | Count passive outgoing projections as authored hyper-act terminal legs, plus the separate raw Profile Reputation component. Reference’s A-leg lands on the citing artifact, which sources its author-fixed T-leg; Reference terminal legs therefore count for every passive type. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:asymmetry`` | remark | An actor-directed act implies no reciprocal act or symmetric parameters. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``subsec:graph:temporal-structure`` | subsection | Track temporal structure as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``ax:graph:act-causality`` | axiom | Treat each accepted complete act as one Lamport event; edge projections inherit its time. The published sequence is the order of record, and recomputed logical time summarizes it. Compliant parents and dependencies have strictly earlier placement. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-incidence-causality`` | definition | Compute one Lamport event per accepted act from its complete incidence: both binary endpoints or the hyper-edge's author, middle and target. Its time is one plus the maximum incident frontier and recursively recomputed times of completed parents and dependencies at lower epoch-position keys. Missing or non-earlier predecessors do not enter the maximum, though placement faults remain. Update only incident frontiers, carry them across boundaries and use zero for genesis or an empty maximum; co-legs share the event time. Authoritative order determines time, never conversely. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:act-incidence-causality-incident-update`` | case | Branch of ``def:graph:act-incidence-causality``: After an act, advance every endpoint in its incidence set to that act frontier; both hyper-edge legs inherit the same event time. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:act-incidence-causality-unchanged-endpoint`` | case | Branch of ``def:graph:act-incidence-causality``: For an endpoint outside an act incidence set, retain its preceding frontier unchanged. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:causal-frontier-not-ancestry`` | remark | A lower recomputed time is necessary but not sufficient for ancestry through correctly placed predecessors or shared incidence. Public parents, declared dependencies, incidence and the published sequence establish the relation; a scalar frontier creates no parent. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:edge-projection-maturity`` | definition | Compute each projection maturity from shared pre-act endpoint degree without sibling self-maturation. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:authoritative-act-order`` | definition | The host publishes a finite total act sequence whose lexicographic epoch-position key determines precedence. Compliance requires completed parents and dependencies at lower keys; violating completed order remains the input for accountability. Hyper-edge legs share a key and logical time is derived from order. Residual ordering discretion can affect results, so agreement is on the sequence rather than only the edge set. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:host-ordering-discretion`` | remark | Publish residual host ordering choices without promising incentive-neutral order. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``prop:graph:act-clock-path-order`` | proposition | Require strictly increasing epoch-position keys between distinct acts along a viable standing path, allowing the internal A-to-T transition of one act at equal key. | 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:act-time-not-wall-clock`` | remark | Compute logical timing and maturity without substituting wall-clock time. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:act-ordering-relations`` | remark | Public causal parents and authoritative order remain distinct from optional dependency-opening availability. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:maturity-max-rationale`` | remark | The canonical maturity uses maximum endpoint degree to retain sparse-to-dense context. | closure | none | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``prop:graph:maturity-bounds`` | proposition | Finite-degree maturity remains in the half-open unit interval and grows with degree. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-payload-state`` | definition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Payload state is a holder’s observation: full-empty has verified empty bytes and the author’s content blinding value, full has other verified bytes and that value, and reduced has neither while the structural commitment remains. Holdings can change through an independently retained opening or local removal; only destruction of information no holder retains is irreversible. Absence at the host establishes neither authored emptiness nor reduction elsewhere; requested intent governs the addressed party’s response, not the act’s state. | courtesy | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

All four combinations preserve the same structural record and witness. Payload authorship is outside what the record establishes about its recorded author. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:rank-two-constraint-restricted-mask`` | case | Branch of ``prop:graph:rank-two-constraint`` proof: Restricted masks inject genuine rank-three content. | 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-removable-projections`` | definition | Model content and dependency commitments and their separately available openings without changing the immutable act. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-dependency-projection`` | definition | Bind one canonical, bounded, duplicate-free dependency set with no self-reference. | 04 Graph | ``case:graph:act-dependency-projection-full-empty`` | case | Within the optional courtesy standard: The holder has the verified canonical empty dependency list and its opening. This observation of holdings is distinct from a missing or tombstoned opening and is never a graph state. | courtesy | serialization | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:act-dependency-projection-full`` | case | Within the optional courtesy standard: A holder has the canonical dependency list and its opening, bound to the act. This describes that holder’s holdings, never a graph state; independently supplied authenticated opening material remains checkable after another holder removes its copy. | courtesy | serialization | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:graph:act-dependency-projection-tombstoned`` | case | Within the optional courtesy standard: The dependency list, the author’s dependency blinding value and other opening material are removed from this holder while the binding commitment remains. This is a holder’s observation, never a graph state or a bar to checking an independently supplied authenticated opening. | courtesy | serialization | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-dependency-tombstone`` | definition | Within the optional courtesy standard: A holder removes the dependency list, the author’s dependency blinding value and other opening material, ending its storage or carriage undertaking without changing the accepted act or finalized structural history. Independently supplied authenticated opening material remains checkable against the unchanged commitment. | courtesy | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:authentication-realization-out-of-scope`` | remark | Leave algorithms, key management and wire realization to the deployment; recorded-signature verification does not establish Actor authority, which is a separate oracle over public history. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:proposal-pre-commitment`` | definition | Before host additions, sign a commitment to the approved structural body, content and dependency values. The body includes its identifier, author, family, incidence, semantic coordinates, protocol references and opaque signed extensions, but no host or order fields. The recorded signature binds those bytes under its key without granting inclusion or Actor authority; the specified construction checks supplied inputs without requiring a content opening. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:verified-act`` | definition | Associate the approved body and signed proposal with host-blinded content and dependency commitments and the host seal. The specified construction determines formation without a separate version field. The seal attests signature and structural checks and commitment formation, not inclusion or content carriage; a failed act check yields no Layer-1 object. Acceptance and ordering do not require possession of a content opening. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:approval-witness`` | definition | Before approval the author checks the seal, exact proposal/body agreement, both commitments under the specified construction and consistent hyper-edge legs. The recorded witness signs that verified association and must verify under its recorded key, without establishing that key's Actor authority. It signs no order or result. Only a validly approved act is eligible for ordering; malformed headers, legs or dependencies refuse, and an unaccepted act stays off-record. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:ordering-fraud-proof`` | definition | Check an authenticated full dependency opening against the completed act, seal, approval and authoritative order. A proof succeeds when a dependency is absent or not at a lower key. The opening reveals the entire list, and verification remains conditional on its authenticated supply. | closure | certificate | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``post:graph:ordering-accountability`` | postulate | Completed authoritative order remains the calculation input even when dependency or parent-placement evidence proves it noncompliant. Such proofs change no records, counts, debits, historical standing or title. Recomputed logical time supplies stamp-fault evidence without rewriting the stamp or reversing consequences. None authorizes refusal of a completed epoch or a replacement-agreement calculation. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``post:graph:act-authentication-requirements`` | postulate | Check recorded signatures, covered-byte integrity, exact approval of host additions, hyper-act unity, retained binding and ordering accountability; external key-to-Actor authority is assessed by a separate oracle and changes no completed calculation. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-signature-verification`` | item | Every recorded proposal and approval signature verifies under the public key the record maps to its exact covered bytes, including the stated author and act identifier; this check does not establish key authority for that Actor. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-proposal-integrity`` | item | Detect replacement of any proposal or approval covered bytes, including the approved body, committed values and opaque signed extensions. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-transparency`` | item | Every host-added field of the finalizable act is within the bytes covered by its recorded approval. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-approval`` | item | Only a finalizable act covered by a well-formed recorded approval is eligible for the authoritative sequence. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-unity`` | item | Require both hyper-edge projections to resolve to the same approved parent act. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-projection-binding`` | item | Retain opening-independent binding of the approved act. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:act-authentication-requirements-ordering`` | item | Where dependency compliance is claimed, retain public evidence that can establish a violation without changing finalized history. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-completion-epoch`` | definition | Identify the first accepted epoch of an act and compare title events at that granularity. | closure | type | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:act-envelope`` | definition | The abstract accepted-act envelope contains its identifier, author, family, structural body, protocol references, public parents, content and dependency commitments, handshake, approval witness, completion epoch and logical time. The handshake and witness include their signature-to-key mappings and verification inputs. Content and blinding values and dependency openings travel separately; serialization and carriage are deployment matters. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:settlement-recognition`` | definition | Check all six recognition clauses on the Bid, Accept and Ratify with buyer, seller and Item derived by ``def:graph:settlement-parties``; include prior ownership, both authors and both targets, and co-epoch cancellation precedence. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-completeness`` | item | Recognize a settlement candidate only if its Bid, Accept and Ratify acts are present with their edge projections in the final epoch edge set. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-reference-binding`` | item | Require both Accept and Ratify settlement references to resolve to the identifier of the Bid act that opened the Offer. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-authorization`` | item | Require the Accept’s author to be the seller and its target the buyer, and the Ratify’s author to be the buyer and its target the seller. Derive the buyer from the Bid’s author and the seller from the certified owner before the Ratify’s epoch, or the genesis owner for a fresh thread, under ``def:graph:settlement-parties``. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-buyer-consent`` | item | Refuse settlement recognition if a buyer Withdraw on the Offer finalizes no later than the Ratify’s epoch; a same-epoch cancellation defeats consent. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-seller-consent`` | item | Refuse settlement recognition if a seller Rescind on the Offer finalizes no later than the Ratify’s epoch; a same-epoch cancellation defeats consent. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``itm:graph:settlement-recognition-well-formedness`` | item | Check the observed handshake order Bid before Accept before Ratify as well-formedness only; it provides no tie breaker. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:title-order-irrelevance`` | remark | Settlement references are causal parents: compliant placement orders Bid, Accept and Ratify by epoch-position key, and correct time recomputation follows. Completed placement or stamp violations are accountability evidence. Conflict resolution uses membership, epochs and the reference-forced chain, never residual sub-epoch ordering or removable dependencies. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``post:graph:boundary-accountability`` | postulate | Publish the dependent-set partition, constraints, asserted timestamp and closure cause so boundary consistency can be checked; no external-clock anchor or unwritten candidate's admission right follows. Publish title equal to the partition fold. A proven title discrepancy is attributable to the host but changes no order or other completed quantity and permits no refusal of the completed epoch. | closure | certificate | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:settlement-clause-structure`` | remark | Recognition clauses (i) and (ii) read membership and pointers; clause (iii) reads prior title and the Bid’s authorship; (iv) and (v) compare completion epochs; (vi) checks the well-formed chain. None reads audit positions, residual sub-epoch order or Lamport comparability. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:ownership-thread`` | definition | Follow boundary transfers from genesis and distinguish failed claims from ordinary description updates. | closure | type | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:settlement-consumption`` | definition | Consume every recognized unchallenged co-epoch candidate permanently even when conflicts prevent transfer. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:title-certificate`` | definition | Map each Item to its terminal thread Actor, or void until its genesis Owner edge completes. Boundary zero is empty and epochs start at one. Refuse epoch-zero acts, out-of-range positions, repeated keys or gaps before replay: they do not form a total order. Publish the recomputable title summary; a differing published value is fault evidence, distinct from malformed order. | closure | certificate | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``thm:graph:epoch-title-fold`` | theorem | Transfer on one candidate to its buyer derived under ``def:graph:settlement-parties``; retain the incumbent on zero or multiple candidates, consuming all conflicting candidates without transfer. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:regret-window`` | remark | The boundary certificate, not Ratify, ends the cancellation opportunity. Either party can cancel co-epochally if included, paying the ordinary debit; completion latency follows the closure timeout and Layer-2 escrows use the certificate. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:cancellation-best-effort`` | remark | A written cancel defeats a settlement candidate only at or before the commit’s completion boundary; a written boundary tie favors cancellation. Submission alone guarantees no delivery, however early, and every cancel still clears the universal write gates. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``thm:graph:settlement-order-freeness`` | theorem | Preserve title under residual sub-epoch permutations of a fixed epoch partition. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``thm:graph:no-death-by-inaction`` | theorem | Require authored settlement transitions rather than per-object expiry. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:causal-locality-principle`` | remark | Permit global progress gates but no settlement state change triggered by its own inactivity. | closure | policy | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:settlement-reputation`` | remark | A terminal policy may interpret the signed settlement stances; it does not change title or standing and cannot establish an external commercial event. The deterministic conjunction is rem:graph:settlement-coordinate-conjunction. | courtesy | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:reviews-commentary`` | remark | Reviews do not change title and resolve their standing allocation home. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:settlement-cross-layer`` | remark | Publish a deterministic title commit anchor without promising custody or payment atomicity. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``alg:graph:title-certificate-computation`` | algorithm | Initialize boundary-zero title to void. At each epoch carry prior title, then enter each Item whose genesis Owner edge completes there with its genesis owner. Transfer to the record-derived buyer for a sole recognized candidate; consume conflicting candidates without transfer. Return the boundary-fold certificate, independent of settlement traversal order. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``subsec:graph:consumer-map`` | subsection | Track consumer map as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``tbl:graph:consumer-map`` | table | Route each geometry output only to its named consumer and keep raw legs out of standing compilation. | closure | policy | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 05 Nodes and edges | ``subsec:nodes:universal-principles`` | subsection | Enforce recorded act ownership and permanent frame addresses, shared time, tensor roles, complete hyper-acts, opaque payloads and census-only allocation; a frame address is not a signing key. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``post:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline`` | postulate | Assign parameter roles, form the masked slice, soften the path view and extract temporal and attribution interfaces. | closure | function | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline-role-assignment`` | step | For every binary edge or hyper-edge leg, assign the domain parameters to directional and intensity roles; for a single-parameter edge set the missing role to one. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline-extended-slice`` | step | Compute each extended tensor slice with the master sentiment-slice formula and its domain bilinear mask. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline-path-view`` | step | Extract the path view from each extended slice using the declared bleed softening. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline-temporal-augmentation`` | step | Augment the extended slice by its outer product with the temporal vector (1, tau). | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:canonical-tensor-pipeline-interface-extraction`` | step | Expose the scalar interface as the extended slice Frobenius norm times sqrt(1+tau squared), the path interface as the extracted path view, and the attribution interface as the unaugmented Frobenius norm. | closure | function | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 05 Nodes and edges | ``prop:nodes:self-loop-neutral`` | proposition | The derived Declaration/Reputation loop cannot improve either raw register. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:self-loop-neutral-max-product`` | step | For raw snapshot traversal, the Declaration/Reputation loop multiplies a register by a product strictly below one, so max-product relaxation cannot improve the source or any mid-path register. Standing does not traverse this loop. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``step:nodes:self-loop-neutral-parity`` | step | The raw Declaration/Reputation loop has positive parity on both edges and maps the initialized positive/negative pair (1,0) to a smaller positive value with zero negative value. Neutrality follows from weight and parity, not a claim that causal order blocks the loop. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-zero`` | item | At zero standing, require zero raw self-edge activation and damped Reputation weight, so the bond is inert. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-monotonicity`` | item | At fixed raw maturity, require the damped Reputation weight to increase strictly with positive standing. This is an input derivative of the raw edge, not a burn-response guarantee. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-bound`` | item | Keep the raw damped Reputation weight strictly below one for every nonnegative standing and permitted maturity. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-concavity`` | item | At fixed raw maturity, require the damped Reputation weight to be strictly concave in positive standing. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-parity`` | item | Require positive Declaration and Reputation determinant parity at every positive standing. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-synchronization`` | item | At every epoch boundary, derive Declaration and Reputation with identical stored tensors, determinant scores and damped-weight formulas. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-can-independence`` | item | Treat normalized CAN as independent of an Actor’s standing only when its Declaration is outside its own composition dependency closure; Reputation contributes nothing to CAN. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-feed-hierarchy`` | item | Bound a raw profile-bridged feed path by the squared Opinion ceiling times the Reputation ceiling; this ceiling is at most half the direct Opinion ceiling. | closure | invariant check | pn-readouts | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 05 Nodes and edges | ``itm:nodes:raw-self-edge-properties-temporal-ceiling`` | item | At frozen raw sentiment, use the maturity-channel supremum equal to the determinant score times the square root of two, strictly below one. | 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:proposed-final-act-state`` | definition | Complete proposed counts and residual balances from the supplied boundary gross amounts, prior state and writing-epoch debit, counting each binary act, complete hyper-edge or Registration once. A gross increment can occur without an act and an act without an increment. Use this same tentative state for standing, emission and gates; retain prior debit vintages and the controller's one-boundary lead. Source-history evaluation is outside the calculation. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:final-action-count`` | equation | Add the number of accepted authored acts once, not projection legs. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``subsec:epoch:standing-principles`` | subsection | Track standing principles as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:standing-is-measurement`` | postulate | Compute standing as a comparison of transported pairs without changing any ledger balance or count; snapshot and compare the ledger across the solve. Standing can nevertheless move or concentrate across alternative complete histories. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:standing-pair-mass-conservation`` | postulate | Conserve full balance and count totals through row-stochastic allocation; this is rival allocation, not invariance of individual standings, participant maxima or unweighted sums. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``thm:epoch:standing-pair-mass-conservation`` | theorem | Conserve full balance and count totals through row-stochastic allocation; this is rival allocation, not invariance of individual standings, participant maxima or unweighted sums. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:standing-epoch-set-determinism`` | postulate | Compile standing from the completed accepted epoch set and derived title result, invariant under dependency-compliant permutations. Authored-vouch withdrawal is netting with no tie rule; settlement consent retains its own epoch-quantized rule. The host’s only lever over standing is the act’s epoch of inclusion, accountable under the write gates; order, position and intra-epoch placement affect no standing quantity. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:tentative-title-fold`` | definition | Resolve the proposed final title state before compiling settlement recipients. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:settlement-standing-title-coupling`` | postulate | Require recognized, unchallenged title transfer and positive folded coordinates for counterparty allocation; canceled, consumed, unauthorized or losing acts resolve to self. A later resale does not revoke a correctly settled earlier transfer. Every accepted act still pays and counts within one source unit; neither inter-family fairness nor a universal standing-response sign follows. Same-author revision nets and withdrawal nets to zero. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:standing-memorylessness`` | postulate | Recompute from current sources without previous-standing inertia. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:universal-act-weighing`` | postulate | Weigh every accepted act once, including control and artifact acts. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:domain-weighted-act-sum`` | postulate | Sum per-domain contributions without averaging away act multiplicity. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:hop-faded-responsive-tilt`` | postulate | Use the published nonincreasing hop profile for recipient responsiveness. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:epoch:cluster-rate-invariance`` | postulate | Preserve the closed transported-count-weighted mean under internal pair allocation; neither an open fibre mean nor individual coordinates or maxima are thereby fixed. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``subsec:epoch:standing-allocation-inputs`` | subsection | Track standing allocation inputs as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:standing-fold-cell`` | definition | Assign accepted acts to the declared complete-act fold cells. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:effective-act-contribution`` | definition | Take the geometric mean of mandatory coordinate magnitudes after folding. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:hostility-is-priced`` | remark | Debit zero or hostile contributions even when they allocate no outward mass. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:standing-recipient-resolution`` | definition | Resolve complete positive person-directed cells to the eligible Actor under the family table, and all other cells to self. Settlement rows read the epoch title fold; Invitation uses positive folded coordinates after netting, with no revocation fold. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:allocation-index-set`` | remark | Retain anchored actors as the standing matrix indices. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:allocation-index-set-unanchored-recipient`` | case | Branch of ``rem:epoch:allocation-index-set``: Keep recipient resolution and allocation on the same finite set of anchored Actors; an unanchored recipient would have a column without a row and could incorrectly receive published standing. Canonical dangling-fold handling alone does not establish this allocation closure. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``tbl:epoch:standing-recipient-resolution`` | table | Route positive Opinion-on-Profile, complete positive Invitation and complete positive Reference-to-Profile to the named Actor; title-transferring Accept and Ratify to the counterparty. Other settlements, controls, artifact-directed acts and hostile or zero person-directed cells resolve home; Participant resolves home in all four cases at its A-leg coefficient. | closure | function | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:standing-domain-profile`` | definition | Assign the declared domain profile to each complete act. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``tbl:epoch:standing-domain-weights`` | table | Use published domain weights and self-retention base. | closure | function | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:self-retention-base`` | definition | Give every source a strictly positive diagonal base. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:domain-weighted-base-allocation`` | definition | Sum weighted cell coefficients by resolved recipient plus self base. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:domain-weighted-base-allocation`` | equation | Sum weighted cell coefficients by resolved recipient plus self base. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:artifact-activity-crowds-out-export`` | remark | Account for self-retention from artifact and control acts; an added act changes debit, count and allocation, so use the complete forcing and both source-rate hulls rather than infer a standing direction. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``subsec:epoch:conserved-standing-transport`` | subsection | Track conserved standing transport as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:standing-tilt-profile`` | definition | Multiply the published hop shape by the selected nonnegative rung. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:hop-allocation-score`` | definition | Tilt base scores by clamped recipient activation. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:mass-owner-relative-hop-order`` | remark | Count hops from the transported source, not backwards from the reader. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:relative-allocation-odds`` | proposition | Check tilted relative odds against the base ratio. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:rival-allocation-response`` | proposition | Check recipient gain and sibling loss derivatives including the safety-wall kink. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:rival-allocation-response`` | equation | Check recipient gain and sibling loss derivatives including the safety-wall kink. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``itm:epoch:rival-allocation-response-rivalry`` | item | Check that allocation derivatives sum to zero in each row: a recipient’s gain is exactly balanced by sibling and self-share losses, with self loss arising from the common denominator. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``itm:epoch:rival-allocation-response-wall-flatness`` | item | Below the safety wall, require zero allocation derivative with respect to that recipient’s standing at every hop; the recipient remains level-discounted rather than dynamically responsive. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``itm:epoch:rival-allocation-response-row-independence`` | item | Keep an allocation row independent of its own Actor’s standing because its self score is unmodulated. Retain indirect diagonal Jacobian terms through other Actors’ rows in the certificate. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:standing-support-preservation`` | proposition | Keep transport support fixed when ledger and compiled base are fixed. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:standing-depth-mass`` | definition | Use nonnegative depth masses summing to one with a positive deepest mass. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:source-emission`` | definition | Cap source emission at its own balance divided by threshold times history count. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:source-emission`` | equation | Set source emission to the minimum of one and residual balance divided by the writing-epoch price times history count. Read the completed ledger and retain the unexported share at self, outside the hop mixture. A fixed-pair partition cannot enlarge its aggregate capacity ceiling, although actual export can move either way. The capacity ceiling coexists with the concentration allowed under the receiving premises of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:source-emission-rationale`` | remark | Apply the fixed source-emission diagonal outside transport, preserving pair mass. A time-decay sink or relocated mass requires a charter amendment and a new cluster-identity argument. Partition capacity is bounded without fixing actual export or standing. Changed-history displacement has the scoped pair and concentration bounds of ``prop:epoch:split-pair-displacement`` and ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:finite-depth-conserved-transport`` | definition | Mix ordered hop-matrix products and add nonemitted mass to the diagonal. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:finite-depth-conserved-transport`` | equation | Mix ordered hop-matrix products and add nonemitted mass to the diagonal. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:transport-is-not-ledger-transfer`` | remark | Transport full pairs for comparison without moving balances, reassigning counts or mutating the ledger. Recorded-source provenance does not imply standing invariance across alternative funded histories. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:emission-self-inertia`` | remark | Retain the unexported share at the source once outside the depth mixture. The self share is at least its full-pair value. Frame gross amount raises own source rate and emission until saturation, not necessarily final standing; rehabilitation requires the stated response tests or a certified finite comparison. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:conserving-emission`` | corollary | Preserve row stochasticity after source emission. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``thm:epoch:emission-export-bound`` | theorem | Bound exported balance and count by the source emission budget. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:emission-export-bound`` | equation | Bound each source’s exported count by the minimum of its count and residual balance over the writing-epoch price; summing gives the cohort balance-over-price ceiling, and zero balance exports none. A fixed-pair partition cannot enlarge the aggregate ceiling, while actual export and standing can change; no universal small displacement follows. The capacity ceiling coexists with the concentration allowed under the receiving premises of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:emission-wall-symmetry`` | remark | Distinguish own-source emission from relational safety. Ledger and emission feedback across epochs does not follow from an in-epoch certificate, and the wall step bound and hysteresis establish no unconditional epoch-to-epoch stability. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:artifact-non-allocation`` | proposition | Check passive-projection invariance only with compiled act inputs held fixed; scope is questioned. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:net-stance`` | definition | Sum complete-act signed coordinates before clipping by author, family, source, target and full incidence, never across authors or independently paired legs. Opinion, Reference and Invitation aggregate regardless of destination, including home-resolving cells. Exclude settlement records, controls and the never-outward families Publish, Owner, Affinity, Tag, Review, Send, Bid, Join Request and Participant by family: excluded authored acts remain singleton cells, while derived Self-edge components form no authored cell. Retain every member and removable payload; raw-record consumers keep their declared inputs. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

Invitation reads folded A-leg u and f, never T-leg relevance; a netted-to-zero Invitation resolves home at coefficient zero, and controls never resolve outward. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:act-folding-properties`` | proposition | Check that cells are author-owned, passive-independent and standing-atomic; preserve incomplete, hostile and self-resolving records for declared non-standing consumers. Equal accepted sets, folds, census and constants compile equal cells, coefficients and recipients. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:folding-precedes-allocation`` | remark | Reconstruct complete acts, apply bundle folds, resolve cells outward or home and emit the base matrix before allocation. Passive artifacts are excluded once in the compiler, not checked again at each hop. Raw feed and signed services traverse the Action Graph independently and read nothing from this matrix. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:control-acts-resolve-to-self`` | remark | Weigh Withdraw, Rescind, Leave and De-invite at self with coefficient one: they have no mandatory stance coordinates and enter no person-vouch projection. De-invite has no standing-visible effect; only the Layer-2 membership fold reads it. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:net-stance-properties`` | proposition | Commutative sum-then-clip makes compilation invariant under dependency-compliant permutations of the accepted epoch set and preserves parameter ranges and existing ceilings. Mixed-sign and coherent-hostile stances fail the positive-coordinate person-vouch gate regardless of raw parity; near-zero net stance is routing-inert. Every revision increments the author’s count and dilutes the commitment rate; immutable records remain while their ongoing effect changes. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:conviction-inertia`` | remark | Keep priced prior contributions in the declared stance fold. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:conviction-inertia-latest-edge`` | case | Branch of ``rem:epoch:conviction-inertia``: Use per-author sum-then-clip rather than latest-edge-wins: reversing accumulated conviction requires proportionate priced counter-acts. A bundle negative in both coordinates is routing-coherent but fails person-vouch eligibility; full withdrawal targets net zero. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:severance-blindness`` | remark | Do not infer unseen revocations or consent from missing records. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``lem:epoch:authored-vouch-withdrawable`` | lemma | Withdraw any authored Opinion, Reference or Invitation by same-family cancellation toward the same incidence in the author’s accepted-epoch bundle. Sum before clipping: exact cancellation fails positivity and weighs home at coefficient zero; a later positive act restores the vouch. Every act remains priced, with no family-specific withdrawal or tie rule; settlements remain singleton cells governed by consent. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
