| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:full-pair-source-transport`` | remark | Transport full source pairs after graduated row emission. Zero-balance sources retain identity rows; partial export obeys the balance-over-price count ceiling. Dilution remains priced and hull-bounded; a hard cutoff is a charter change, and the cap promises no small response or fading prevalence. A fixed-pair partition cannot enlarge the aggregate capacity ceiling, although actual export and target standing may change. The capacity ceiling coexists with the concentration allowed under the receiving premises of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:final-standing-hull`` | proposition | Enclose each coordinate in its contributing source-rate hull; funded support concentration can approach a whole hull width, so the hull is not a small strategic-gain guarantee. The same concentration record can describe a legitimately separate role under ``ex:epoch:split-showcase``; its magnitude does not reveal purpose. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:equal-rate-standing-invariance`` | proposition | Equal contributing rates give that exact standing; heterogeneous partitions do not inherit this invariant. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:no-tilt-anchor`` | proposition | Evaluate the zero-tilt state without a nonlinear fixed-point iteration. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``thm:epoch:closed-cluster-rate-identity`` | theorem | Check the conserved pooled-rate identity for a closed cluster. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:closed-cluster-rate-identity`` | equation | Check the conserved pooled-rate identity for a closed cluster. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:closed-cluster-mean`` | equation | For a closed cluster, the transported-count-weighted mean of final standing equals aggregate residual frame balance divided by aggregate action count. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:conserving-source-root-criterion`` | corollary | Check cluster support closure for the pooled-rate identity. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:cluster-rate-leakage`` | corollary | With boundary base-allocation weight at most epsilon in either direction, departure from the closed-cluster mean is bounded by that leakage times the rate width. This weighted-mean bound gives no individual frame gross response sign. Intact pair totals and the closed weighted mean can remain conserved while partition changes individual coordinates or maxima. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:actor-partition-comparison`` | definition | Declare matched complete histories, participant fibre, pair partition, fixed wall/numeraire/policy and recipient/title premises; each history selects its greatest passing rung. This is an explicit counterfactual, not reassignment or inferred common ownership. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | type | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-pair-displacement`` | proposition | Check centred pair displacement over the new positive denominator and its common-source rate-width times total-variation bound; changed counts alone do not determine movement. The receiving regime admits the refinement of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-exact-invariants`` | proposition | Preserve transported pair totals, the closed transported-count-weighted mean and equal-rate coordinates under the stated partitions; individual coordinates, unweighted sums and maxima need not agree. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-fibre-capacity`` | proposition | Bound actual external export by the summed member capacities, no larger than the old capacity. Proportional partition preserves the ceiling, not actual export. The self-path ceiling in ``prop:epoch:split-self-path-export`` refines this capacity bound without turning it into a global advantage optimum. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-self-path-export`` | proposition | Refine fibre export using certified positive diagonal stay-path probabilities over published depth masses; attainment in the absorbing anchor fixture is not global optimality. Sharpness is confined to its stated family; ``prop:epoch:split-fibre-capacity`` controls the general capacity. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` | proposition | Bound participant maximum gain by member displacement and a common hull width; with unchanged incoming support and absorbing equal-rate members, check the exact concentration formula and its conditional continuous envelope. The identical receiving-family record also permits legitimate separation under the hypotheses of ``prop:epoch:split-legitimate-completion`` and ``ex:epoch:split-showcase``. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence`` | theorem | For two semantic completions with identical public acts, payloads, ledgers, constants and all rule inputs, deterministic outputs coincide and randomized outputs have the same law when conditional randomness does. A record-based remedy imposes the same standing or fixed-context terminal change on legitimate separation and strategic concentration. This compares completions of one record, not pooled and partitioned records. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-legitimate-completion`` | proposition | In the admitted receiving family, partition N Registrations into n and N-n at equal source rate with 0<n<N; keep independent donors, complete positive Opinions, incoming acts, absorbing participant rows, empty titles and greatest passing rungs. A legitimate completion exists when private roles are assignable independently, payloads make no contradictory aggregate-scope claim, donors can intend the retained role and no extra public identity or purpose attestation intervenes. This is an existence result for that family, not an inference of actual intent or a claim about every strategic history. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``ex:epoch:split-small`` | example | Reproduce the small certified receiving fixture under ``ver:standing:split-receiving`` and its legitimate-completion hypotheses. Its one partitioned public record admits both an endorsed-role separation and strategic concentration. A record-based reduction or consumer exclusion affects both equally, or with the same distribution if randomized; pooled and partitioned standings need not agree. | closure | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``ex:epoch:split-showcase`` | example | Reproduce the certified near-hull concentration fixture under ``ver:standing:split-concentration`` and its legitimate-completion hypotheses. The same sparse retained Profile may separate an endorsed role from unrelated maintenance or serve strategic concentration. A record-based remedy imposes the same loss on both completions, or the same loss distribution; near-hull displacement is no evidence of private purpose. | closure | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-registration-cost`` | proposition | Count the additional Registration debit at its writing price; this is not incremental aggregate frame gross amount between matched histories, which may be zero. Under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``, a record-based remedy imposes the same change on a legitimate completion with identical public fields, or the same distribution of changes if randomized. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-centered-invariance`` | proposition | Check all centred residuals at the lifted coarse solution within the refined hull; simultaneous vanishing characterizes invariance at that state, not a structural classification before solving. The all-state criterion of ``thm:dynamics:split-hop-refinement`` has stronger premises than this equilibrium test. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:split-admission-clearance`` | proposition | Use matched-author standing bounds and their act-weighted mean to preserve predicates only when clearance exceeds them; separately check formation, solvency and capacity. Existing fixtures demonstrate no verdict flip. The comparisons in ``ver:standing:split-admissibility`` certify both complete histories; they do not infer private purpose. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:final-standing-response`` | proposition | With source rates and other transported coefficients fixed, increasing a source’s contribution lifts or dilutes the target according as its rate exceeds or falls below the target’s standing. A complete act or frame gross amount changes further inputs and needs the full finite-response comparison. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:final-standing-embedding`` | proposition | Embed disconnected subsystems without changing their standing. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:author-directed-allocation`` | remark | Allocate only through the author whose act expresses regard. A relay apportions the original source’s unit, never its own; no passive artifact supplies authority or target veto. Frozen-flow source-rate comparisons give no universal sign for the completed act. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:dilution-cost`` | proposition | Charge each dilution act the in-force price: n such acts cost n times that price, and total grip is bounded by spent balance divided by price. Row conservation prevents cross-target copying. Funded partition and coupled frame gross amount or act responses remain subject to capacity, shock and hull bounds, not immunity or a universal sign. Changed-history displacement has the scoped pair and concentration bounds of ``prop:epoch:split-pair-displacement`` and ``prop:epoch:split-concentration``. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:griefing-resistance`` | remark | Bound griefing by author-only rows, priced and count-diluting acts, one rival unit per source, and the depleted-source export ceiling. Low-rate support may still dilute without a target veto; even an unreached sibling can decrease through rival feedback, within the stated response bounds. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:rate-parking`` | remark | Do not infer a universal rate-parking prohibition beyond the declared premises. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:no-tilt-commitment-monotonicity`` | proposition | At zero tilt with counts, base, threshold and emission fixed, reached coordinates increase under frame gross amount and unreached coordinates stay fixed. Changing emission invalidates this premise; positive-tilt reversals are certified. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``thm:epoch:frame-gross-response`` | theorem | Under the chartered tilt profile, depth masses, positive self-retention and a published wall at or above its structural floor, passing certificates do not imply monotonic standing under a supplied frame gross increment or an accepted act. There are admissible saturated frame gross comparisons at one fixed positive accepted rung in which the source actor decreases, a reached sibling decreases, and a structurally unreached sibling decreases. These are separate fixtures, not a claim that every ledger has every effect. There is also a depleted-source frame gross comparison with a decreasing reached recipient at the anchor. For selected rungs there is no finite universal upper bound on standing loss divided by a positive frame gross increment. One additional accepted act without additional frame gross amount has no universal direction; in the ordinary regime without an additional finite source ceiling its absolute own-standing loss is unbounded across admissible ledgers even when both contributing widths are zero. The fixed-rung losses in the certified fixtures are small in absolute size; the own feedback can nevertheless exceed the source actor's own direct forcing. One act can remove a substantial fraction of an isolated Actor's standing. The bounds are ``prop:epoch:frame-gross-response-bound``, ``prop:epoch:finite-standing-response``; the surviving positive regimes are ``cor:epoch:frame-gross-monotone-regimes``. This result changes neither the certificate nor the selection rule. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:frame-gross-response-bound`` | proposition | At a smooth saturated fixed passing rung, feedback offsets at most two thirds of the largest direct burn forcing, not necessarily of the burner own forcing; preserve the fixed counts, base, wall and numeraire premises. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:frame-gross-monotone-regimes`` | corollary | Establish positive response only by the stated direct-forcing versus feedback tests, fixed-emission anchor or sole-source premises. Small-rung thresholds depend on the ledger; finite paths require the premises throughout. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:finite-standing-response`` | proposition | Bound two certified completed states on the same Actor set by their full forcing and both hulls; recompute emission, folds and rung for changed acts or frame gross amounts instead of assuming a direction. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:shared-frame-gross-snapshot-rationale`` | remark | Use one fixed supplied boundary snapshot for the accepted calculation; source recognition and external history are separate premises. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:raw-self-edge-reading`` | equation | Derive current Self bonds and tenure from final standing without appending records. | closure | function | pn-readouts | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:dense-cluster-fairness`` | remark | As incoming transported action mass dominates own retained mass, standing approaches the contributing support’s weighted rate; density or headcount alone does not establish that limit. Identical public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration under ``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``; private purpose is not a record-derived input. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:write-own-balance-solvency`` | condition | Continuously require the recorded author's residual balance to cover the price in force; only that author's own balance pays the debit on a write. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:relational-gate-vs-own-export`` | remark | Keep solvency, own-history emission and relational safety as separate predicates. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:admission-statistics-quarantine`` | remark | Exclude demand-sensitive regime and headroom from unit-side formulas. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:consent-exposure`` | remark | A cancel written through the commit’s epoch defeats it and wins a boundary tie; suppression leaves no Layer-1 trace and submission alone guarantees no delivery. Boundary-censorship exposure requires a mechanism with declared selection and availability properties, not an audit claim about unwritten demand. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:band-dwell`` | remark | For an unendorsed Actor with no new frame gross amount, the exact own-source formulas give door and wall crossing and the stated band-dwell benchmarks, subject to headroom. | 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:non-written-dynamic-live`` | remark | Retain nonwritten actors in current dynamics unless the safety clamp applies. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:registration-final-set-admission`` | remark | Apply ordinary one-act debit, count, standing and gate rules to Registration. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:same-epoch-source-hull`` | corollary | A same-epoch cohort whose contributing source rates all lie below the wall cannot vouch itself above it; a closed zero-rate cohort stays at zero without external positive-rate source. The source hull creates no source and supplies no general heterogeneous funded-partition invariance. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:universal-frame-amount-benchmarks`` | corollary | Compute the ``cond:epoch:write-own-balance-solvency``, safety and door burn benchmarks without promising admission. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``subsec:epoch:floor-governance`` | subsection | Track floor governance as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:safety-threshold`` | definition | The reduced wall is primary and the charged price is its numeraire image; certify that charge on its own epoch width. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-derivability`` | condition | Derive the next wall from the completed certificate and published constants, then publish its price image. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-in-epoch-certification`` | condition | Certify the greatest attempted rung whose supplied or recoverable sound enclosure support proves PASS on the epoch own width; retain higher attempts and distinguish proved failure from indecision. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-wall-step-bound`` | condition | Bound a wall change by the lesser of the positive absolute cap and the relative cap times the previous wall. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-wall-hysteresis`` | condition | Apply a symmetric relative deadband to the floored proposal before the step cap, with no hidden latch. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-publication-lead`` | condition | Publish the next wall and price one boundary ahead without repricing a consummated debit. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-controller-publication`` | condition | Publish the wall in force, controller formula and constants, proposal, next wall and its frame amount image beside the standing certificate, accepted rung and width. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-numeraire-covariance`` | condition | Keep the controller blind to the numeraire and preserve reduced inputs under recoinage; distinguish other structural changes. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-declared-floor`` | condition | Certify the actual wall at or above the deployment fixed exact floor, including zero width and an empty domain. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:epoch:safety-input-quarantine`` | condition | Read only previous wall, completed width, accepted rung and constants; exclude admission demand and private history. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:policy-floor`` | definition | Announce the host floor one boundary ahead and reject values below the led safety floor. | closure | function | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:floor-validity`` | equation | Check the policy floor against the dimensionless safety floor. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:participation-floor`` | definition | Take the maximum of policy and safety floors as fault totalization. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``eq:epoch:effective-floor`` | equation | Compute the derived maximum floor without treating it as a new dial. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:safety-wall`` | definition | Publish the reduced wall with one-boundary lead and derive its price by multiplication by the numeraire. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:safety-wall-floor`` | definition | Declare once beside the numeraire either the exact clean-elasticity ridge image or the rational three thirty-sevenths; compare the actual reduced wall against that fixed structural floor. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:safety-wall-floor-ridge-floor`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:safety-wall-floor``: The ridge-floor choice is the exact real p-star/(1-p-star) defined by the unique elasticity ridge, not a grid endpoint or rounded root. Declare it publicly before the first epoch and keep the structural declaration fixed unless a public structural change regenerates its evidence. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:safety-wall-floor-rational-floor`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:safety-wall-floor``: The alternative floor is the declared exact rational value from the chartered two-element floor set. Publish the choice before the first epoch beside the numeraire; changing it or choosing a third value requires a public structural change with its own evidence, never epoch tuning. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cor:epoch:floor-economics`` | corollary | A wall at or above the positive structural floor gives a price at least numeraire times that floor and bounds real remaining-action capacity per frame gross unit by its reciprocal. Apply integer flooring to a residual balance. Full emission requires a source rate at least the delivered wall; floor attainment is a mathematical bound, not a cap on every wall. | closure | none | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``rem:epoch:floor-is-a-landmark`` | remark | Treat the floor as a structural leverage choice, not a consequence of in-epoch certification; the exact defining object determines the comparison. | closure | none | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:wall-controller`` | definition | Publish a numeraire-blind memory-one controller of previous wall, completed width and accepted rung; exclude demand, hidden state and width forecasts. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller`` | definition | Compute a floored full-strength target, apply the relative deadband, then cap the signed step by absolute and relative bounds; zero width uses the floor target. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller-zero-width`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller``: At zero completed-epoch width, propose the declared structural floor without inversion or division by width; the delivered wall still passes through deadband and step-cap handling. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller-positive-width`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller``: At positive completed width W, propose the maximum of the structural floor and inverse deployed gain at 1/(2 times margin times chartered cumulative tilt times W). This targets the least mathematically passing wall; finite boundary comparisons may be indecisive and the next epoch certifies its own width. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller-deadband-hold`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller``: When the target error magnitude is at most deadband times the previous wall, set the effective error to zero and hold the wall, including equality. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller-deadband-move`` | case | Branch of ``def:epoch:canonical-interim-wall-controller``: Outside the relative deadband, retain the signed target error and move by at most its magnitude, the absolute step and the relative step times the previous wall. Apply proposal, floor, deadband and cap in that order; publish proposal and delivered wall with one-boundary lead. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:safety-independent-of-controller`` | proposition | Under the conserved-map ledger, support and invariant-box hypotheses, every finite wall at or above its structural floor has a greatest proved passing rung on the completed epoch’s own width, with a unique fixed point and geometric convergence from its box. This does not require a correct controller forecast or attainment of the chartered rung, and does not discharge the separate write and policy tests. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:safety-independent-of-controller-nonempty-domain`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:safety-independent-of-controller`` proof: On a nonempty finite live domain under the standing hypotheses, a positive finite wall gives finite gain, and the finite grid includes the zero-certificate anchor. The nonempty finite set of proved passes has a greatest rung; its contraction gives unique standing and geometric convergence without a next-epoch width-dominance premise. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:safety-independent-of-controller-empty-domain`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:safety-independent-of-controller`` proof: On an empty live domain, the standing box contains only the empty vector, which is the unique fixed point. This does not discharge own-balance, individual-safety, epoch-admission or policy-validity tests. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-total`` | proposition | Define empty-domain width as zero and otherwise use the maximum contributing source-rate range. On every finite declared input triple, the canonical interim controller must return a finite proposal and delivered wall at or above the floor, obeying the step bound. Zero width proposes the floor, and a wall already there stays there. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-covariance`` | proposition | Under recoinage, co-scale frame gross amounts and numeraire while retaining reduced constants and counts: reduced rates, standing, width and wall stay unchanged, the next price co-scales, and old debits change representation only. A fixed-ledger numeraire migration instead evaluates the controller on its published transformed inputs; same-pair width scales inversely, compensation preserves the door product, an uncompensated move keeps its declared policy schedule, and floor and policy tests still apply. No controller homogeneity or solvency and individual-safety invariance is promised for that migration. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:wall-controller-covariance-recoinage`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-covariance``: Co-scale every frame-denominated value and the numeraire by positive c while retaining reduced constants and counts. Reduced rates, standing, width and wall stay unchanged and the next price scales by c. Historical debits change representation, not economic charge. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:wall-controller-covariance-fixed-ledger-migration`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-covariance``: Under a numeraire migration by positive c at a fixed frame amount ledger, evaluate the controller on the supplied transformed wall, width and rung, then multiply by the new numeraire for price. The same fixed source pairs have width W/c; this is not a forecast for differently debited acts. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:wall-controller-covariance-compensated`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-covariance``: Compensated fixed-ledger migration also divides the policy floor by c so the numeraire-door product remains invariant. Validate the published wall floor and policy values; the product identity does not assert controller homogeneity or invariant write gates. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:epoch:wall-controller-covariance-uncompensated`` | case | Branch of ``prop:epoch:wall-controller-covariance``: Uncompensated fixed-ledger migration retains its declared policy schedule. Apply wall-floor and policy-validity tests to the published values; do not assume controller homogeneity or invariance of own-balance and individual-safety tests. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``def:epoch:final-act-stamps`` | definition | Give every same-author act the final standing stamp and aggregate once per act. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

Compensating the policy floor preserves its door product without preserving every validity margin. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 7 Sorting | ``thm:sorting:monte-carlo-concentration`` | theorem | Establish a deterministic positive finite support bound independently of observed samples, positive selection probability for every positive-weight eligible path, and independent walks under one fixed stopped law before applying Hoeffding concentration. | 8 Transport | ``post:transport:guild-grant`` | postulate | Permit terminal guild reinterpretations within the declared grant constraints, without changing binding standing or inferring that funding increases standing or preserves terminal context. Partition readout bounds require the matched creators and fixed non-standing context of ``prop:transport:split-terminal-readout-bounds``. Richer terminal sign transport must publish its transported object, composition and deterministic replay under ``subsec:deployment:completeness``; order-sensitive rotations inherit no determinant path factorization and cannot change binding standing or admission. Consume published title as the recomputable summary protected by title-certificate fault accountability; recomputation audits it and grants no override right. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``rem:transport:guild-grant-preconditions`` | remark | Guilds may reinterpret terminal commercial assertions but cannot replace the title fold or standing recipient rule; unrecognized, challenged or non-transferring settlements resolve to self, without a promised terminal remedy. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases`` | remark | Guild choices are terminal reimplementations with public rules and canonical defaults; every CAN replacement retains depth decay, transmission below one and context independence without changing binding values or closure. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:membership-policy`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: Choose a published terminal membership and sorting policy; the canonical fold admits participation and departure; De-invites are advisory unless recognized by a published policy, without conditioning admission, departure or De-invite authoring. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:deep-composition`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: Choose composition depth and scalar or matrix representation; the default scalar CAN follows the dependency DAG, and every choice preserves depth decay, transmission below one and context independence. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:svd-corrected-transmission`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: Optionally replace saturated mean edge norms by the saturated top singular value of the mean matrix; the full recursion must retain the CAN invariants. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:can-aggregation-function`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: Choose the aggregation function; arithmetic mean is canonical, while alternatives must preserve depth decay, strict transmission and context independence. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:bridge-multi-bridge`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: Choose public bridge composition and attribution; the canonical single-bridge rule takes the outer maximum per recipient, and replacements must re-establish any claimed canonical guarantees. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 8 Transport | ``case:transport:bridge-eligibility-cliff`` | case | Branch of ``rem:transport:guild-grant-cases``: A guild chooses smoothing, with the unsmoothed single-bridge maximum as canonical default; a replacement accounts for redundancy amplification and inherits or re-derives each canonical guarantee it claims. Separately, the centralized-phase operator publishes an argmax-flip-rate threshold and a share-volatility companion, the ℓ1 distance between consecutive normalized share vectors, both computable from epoch-certificate data. Exceeding the threshold obliges the operator to publish a reference top-k ensemble composition rule; its use remains terminal and optional under the guild grant. This publication is a required policy; the implementation home is held in (`open:inventory:bridge-reaction-placement`). | closure | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| A Gates | ``app:gates`` | appendix | Track gates as a separately addressable appendix. | closure | none | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:split-scope`` | remark | For an unsupported Actor sustaining reduced rate c, each accepted act requires c times numeraire plus the action price in new frame gross amount; wall-neutral sustaining frame gross amount is twice that price. Relational support may help stamps but never reduces the own-balance debit. Additional Registration debit and incremental aggregate frame gross amount are distinct, and cost alone bounds no small standing gain. Use the separate Registration-debit and incremental-frame gross amount denominators of ``prop:epoch:split-registration-cost``. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``subsec:gates:door-and-wall`` | subsection | Track door and wall as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``thm:gates:sustaining`` | theorem | An unendorsed Actor preserving reduced own rate c across an accepted act needs exactly c times the numeraire plus the action price in new frame gross amount. At the safety wall this is twice the price; at the door it is the door rate's frame image plus the debit. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``cor:gates:band-floor`` | corollary | Check that unsupported wall-neutral activity needs twice the action threshold. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:propped`` | remark | Never let relational standing pay another actor's solvency debit. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``subsec:gates:frontier`` | subsection | Track frontier as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``thm:gates:frontier`` | theorem | Check the unsupported long-run sustaining lower bounds under their fixed-floor premises. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``prop:gates:two-phase-transit`` | proposition | For an unendorsed Actor acting without new frame amount, the stated exact action-count formulas give door and safety-wall crossing and band dwell, subject to headroom. Re-entry needs additional amount, not elapsed time. These own-source formulas do not predict a relational stamp without replaying its standing package. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:frontload`` | remark | Front-loading supplied frame amount can delay an unendorsed Actor's door or wall crossing without lowering its long-run own-source sustaining average. Acquisition timing and price lie below Layer 1; relational standing can alter stamps but not the own-balance debit. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``subsec:gates:lever`` | subsection | Track lever as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``cor:gates:standing-transport-no-solvency-discount`` | corollary | Keep every accepted actor's own threshold debit unchanged by transport. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:tilt-is-not-price-discount`` | remark | Lowering responsiveness does not alter the debit, numeraire or policy floor. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``thm:gates:equal-rate-no-internal-lift`` | theorem | When every contributing pair, including the own pair, has one common reduced rate, final reduced standing equals that rate under every certified allocation. This exact invariance does not extend to heterogeneous funded partitions. The receiving-family concentration of ``prop:epoch:split-concentration`` remains compatible with these scoped invariants. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:external`` | remark | Bound relational assistance by contributing source rates rather than a fixed discount. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``subsec:gates:funding`` | subsection | Track funding as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:guild-funding`` | remark | Supplied funding retains its opaque Actor attribution. Additional amount can change residual balance, but standing and terminal effects require their separate response and fixed-context comparisons; funding neither freezes allocation nor chooses an uncertified rung. Funded partitions still pay each Registration debit even when matched histories have zero aggregate frame increment. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:standing-safety-scope`` | remark | Separate own-balance solvency, individual and epoch gates, contraction and movement; conserve transported pairs without transferring ledger assets. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| A Gates | ``rem:gates:sustaining-denomination`` | remark | Keep the numeraire conversion, literal action debit, policy membrane, safety wall and tilt rung distinct. | D Dynamics | ``eq:dynamics:tilt-profile-prior-screen`` | equation | Use cumulative tilt and width dominance only as a sufficient screen; a proposed weighted refinement needs its own sound bound and charter adoption. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``rem:dynamics:screen-cap-is-derived`` | remark | Derive the cap from rivalry factor and activation exponent rather than an independent tunable value. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``rem:dynamics:chartered-decay-determined`` | remark | Preserve the chartered halving shape and supported-depth slack; alternatives require published admissible standing parameters and regenerated comparison evidence. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``def:dynamics:conserved-standing-certificate`` | definition | Publish the evaluated fence scalar and its margined pass verdict. | closure | function | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``rem:dynamics:certificate-is-the-fence`` | remark | Use the evaluated width fence; a graph-sensitive global enclosure is conditional on a stronger charter and demonstrated benefit, not an interchangeable second gate. Contraction controls amplification of a measured residual, not the size of changed allocation under a partition. For a changed partition, apply the measured projected residual of ``prop:dynamics:split-projected-residual``; contraction alone does not make the input shock small. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``prop:dynamics:emission-certificate-transfer`` | proposition | Keep the ledger-derived emission diagonal fixed throughout the solve. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``lem:dynamics:standing-mean-deviation`` | lemma | Bound the Jacobian by half the un-margined certificate using source-rate mean deviation on the nonnegative domain; keep the original evaluated fence and rung-selection test unchanged. For a changed partition, apply the measured projected residual of ``prop:dynamics:split-projected-residual``; contraction alone does not make the input shock small. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``prop:dynamics:standing-response-resolvent`` | proposition | Bound local sensitivity by the inverse Jacobian resolvent, certified nonnegative majorants or signed-series remainder; use direct own forcing rather than possibly zero net response as the relative denominator. For a changed partition, apply the measured projected residual of ``prop:dynamics:split-projected-residual``; contraction alone does not make the input shock small. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``prop:dynamics:standing-finite-response`` | proposition | Compare separately certified states on the same Actor set using full forcing, both hulls and optional certified corridor matrix bounds; changed Actor sets require an explicit comparison map. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``prop:dynamics:standing-intervention-forcing`` | proposition | Include the signed emission derivative below the wall and one-sided kink cases; for finite frame gross amount, rung or act changes recompute the complete fold, debit, count and transport before applying the response bound. | closure | invariant check | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``cor:dynamics:minimal-safe-rung`` | corollary | At positive contributing width W, a rung passes exactly when cumulative tilt is at most 1/(2 times margin times gain times W); at zero width every rung passes. The anchor is exact and acceptance uses the greatest attempted PASS, which may be below a mathematically passing indecisive rung. Clean profile-cap wall diagnostics must not substitute their tilt volume for the deployed profile. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``case:dynamics:minimal-safe-wall-positive-width`` | case | Branch of ``cor:dynamics:minimal-safe-rung`` proof: At positive width, the evaluated certificate 2 times gain times cumulative tilt times width passes exactly when cumulative tilt is at most the reciprocal of 2 times margin times gain times width; the division is valid only in this positive-width branch. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``case:dynamics:minimal-safe-wall-zero-width`` | case | Branch of ``cor:dynamics:minimal-safe-rung`` proof: At zero width, the evaluated rung certificate is zero by multiplication, without division by width; every rung passes this certificate. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``case:dynamics:minimal-safe-wall-anchor`` | case | Branch of ``cor:dynamics:minimal-safe-rung`` proof: At the zero-tilt anchor, cumulative tilt and the certificate are zero at every width, so the anchor passes independently of clean-screen profile-cap inversion. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``rem:dynamics:wall-tilt-tradeoff`` | remark | Distinguish the within-epoch wall/tilt certificate tradeoff from conditional inter-epoch controller models and measured response. | closure | invariant check | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``thm:dynamics:finite-max-envelope-lipschitz`` | theorem | Retain finite-max Lipschitz bounds for raw diagnostics, outside standing selection. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``prop:dynamics:sibling-response-without-path`` | proposition | A positive allocation to a sibling gives a negative cross-coordinate response absent from a direct selected path, obstructing entrywise domination. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``rem:dynamics:certified-bound-scope`` | remark | The evaluated hop-profile width fence bounds the conserved Jacobian; the selected-path circulant extremum supplies no conserved-map upper bound, and the zero-tilt equality case is not a bifurcation onset. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| D Dynamics | ``subsec:dynamics:quarantined-apparatus-scope`` | subsection | Restrict smooth arguments to fixed responsive cells; selected-path oscillators and approximate-solver drift establish no conserved-map onset. Failed certification proves neither contraction nor divergence; controller response is a deployment evidence duty. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| G Frontiers | ``app:frontiers`` | appendix | Inventory four architectural extensions with their scope, requirements and stake. No entry meets the Layer-1 open-problem criterion; an extension does not license a change to the operative charter. | closure | none | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| G Frontiers | ``def:frontiers:open-problem`` | definition | An open problem must be capable of changing a needed admission, faithfulness or convergence claim or requirement; an already proved bound or a deployment choice with fixed validation is not one merely because it could be sharpened. The appendix contains four architectural extensions and no Layer-1 open problem. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| G Frontiers | ``rem:frontiers:priority`` | remark | Prioritize exact conserved evaluation and the boundary mechanism. Adopted exact reuse retains its comparison evidence; the mechanism also needs its agreement, selection and liveness arguments. Accepted evidence follows replay verification, and a replacement certificate requires the specified stronger charter. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| G Frontiers | ``subsec:frontiers:core`` | subsection | No appendix entry meets the Layer-1 open-problem criterion. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| G Frontiers | ``subsec:frontiers:architectural`` | subsection | Distinguish the boundary mechanism and exact conserved evaluation from the two conditional charter extensions. The mechanism owes agreement, selection and availability; optional exact reuse owes equality and measured benefit. Richer terminal sign transport is governed by the guild grant and formula completeness, not an architectural frontier. Four architectural extensions leave no Layer-1 open problem. | closure | policy | pn-replay | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| G Frontiers | ``frontier:boundary-mechanism`` | frontier (arch) | Specify the submission model, candidate class, assumptions and testable selection guarantee. Establish agreement on the published act sequence, placement, causally closed edge set and complete calculation package; recompute logical time rather than agree it. Supply search policy, trial and validation budgets and fallback; optional search measurements do not replace a verdict, and no timeout permits an invalid accepted set. Written-side accountability proves neither treatment of unwritten candidates nor decentralized selection. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; environment |
| G Frontiers | ``frontier:high-dimensional-sentiment`` | frontier (arch) | Changing the operative tensor or temperature requires charter adoption with rank, determinant, composition, replay and multivariate-maturity evidence; extra coordinates alone establish none of these. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; environment |
| G Frontiers | ``frontier:exact-conserved-evaluation`` | frontier (arch) | Implement exact conserved evaluation against declared scale, hardware, memory and boundary-duration envelopes; publish limiting load and failure mode. Adopted reuse must revalidate current inputs and reproduce complete recomputation’s certified package and verdicts under the specified calculation. Retain every required act and relation; equality of fixed-point certification requires neither a published compiler-version field nor identical iteration history, and numerical allowances cannot select verdicts. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; environment |
| G Frontiers | ``frontier:certificate-enclosure`` | frontier (arch) | Only a specified stronger charter can propose a global replacement certificate: state the required rung and graph class the width fence cannot certify, prove soundness including wall kinks, and report cost, interval overestimation or equality. The operative fence remains authoritative; no implementation chooses whichever gate passes. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; environment |

| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:universal-parallelism-uniqueness-constraint`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:universal-parallelism``: Keep all record families parallelizable with no Layer-1 merge or supersession; refuse one-record-per-author/target or per-actor uniqueness constraints, which pull per-family state machines into admission and destroy the parallel accumulation needed for priced walk-back. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:universal-parallelism-latest-wins`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:universal-parallelism``: Keep binding standing and title bundle readings distinct from terminal consumers’ fold choices; refuse Layer-1 latest-wins everywhere, which repeals sum-then-clip, makes stance free to flip and imposes one fold on consumers with different needs. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:payload-isolation`` | subsection | Separate removable payload from retained structural commitment. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:payload-isolation-joint-commitment`` | case | Refuse committing structure and payload jointly: structural authentication must remain possible when the separable payload opening is unavailable; binding and concealment rest on the declared cryptographic premise. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:pre-commitment-before-salting`` | subsection | The author’s pre-commitment precedes host-added blinding values | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:pre-commitment-before-salting-host-first`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:pre-commitment-before-salting``: Keep the Actor’s signed proposal pre-commitment before host blinding values; refuse host-first commitments with only a final Actor signature, which leave no cryptographic trace of endpoints, content or dependencies substituted before the host’s contribution. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:approval-after-salting`` | subsection | The author approves only after the host-added blinding values fix the returned commitments. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:approval-after-salting-no-approval`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:approval-after-salting``: Keep Actor approval of the exact verified act with host blinding values before admission; refuse admitting a host-constructed verified act without that second signature, which binds the Actor to content or dependencies they never opened or checked. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:act-level-dependencies`` | subsection | Address dependencies to complete acts with one projection per act. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:act-level-dependencies-leg-dependencies`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:act-level-dependencies``: Keep dependencies at act level with one dependency projection and shared parent act for both hyper-edge legs; refuse separate per-leg lists and ordering, which can split an atomic act across another act or bind a provable dependency list to only one leg. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:authoritative-order`` | subsection | Publish one agreed total act order rather than independent local linearizations. | closure | invariant check | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:authoritative-order-local-linearization`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:authoritative-order``: Keep one published authoritative total act order; refuse publishing only the accepted set and letting readers linearize locally, which makes raw maturities, projected times and path eligibility diverge. | H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:retained-commitment`` | subsection | The retained commitment binds without its opening; no particular tombstoning event is needed. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:retained-commitment-remove-residue`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:retained-commitment``: Keep the binding dependency commitment residue after removing the list and opening; refuse deleting the residue too, which leaves a later fraud proof nothing to open against and permits fabricated lists or denial of the committed list. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``subsec:necessity:title-order-separation`` | subsection | Use authoritative order for handshake formation and epoch membership for conflict outcomes. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| H Necessity | ``case:necessity:title-order-separation-host-title-tiebreak`` | case | Refused simplification under ``subsec:necessity:title-order-separation``: Keep Bid-before-Accept-before-Ratify well-formedness under authoritative order while title conflict resolution reads epoch membership; refuse using residual sub-epoch host order to choose the winning commit, which gives the operator a title-fabrication lever. | closure | invariant check | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``app:deployment`` | appendix | Track deployment as a separately addressable appendix. | courtesy | none | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``subsec:deployment:public-availability`` | subsection | Describe optional ways to make accepted records and replay support available; delivery is distinct from the complete inputs required once replay is attempted. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-streaming`` | case | Optional operating example: Streaming the append-log through pub/sub satisfies public availability when every participant can retrieve the public act records, projections and standing-package data required for independent verification; streaming minimizes cache staleness. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-api-polling`` | case | Optional operating example: Open API polling with pagination is a public-availability option: it introduces bounded staleness without breaking the requirement that participants can retrieve the records and standing-package data needed for verification. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-snapshot-delta`` | case | Optional operating example: Periodic snapshots with delta encoding are a public-availability option: bounded staleness is permitted while participants retain access to the records and standing-package data needed for verification. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-gossip`` | case | Optional operating example: P2P gossip replication satisfies public availability when it makes the public records and required standing-package data continuously retrievable for independent verification. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-gated-access`` | case | Optional operating example: Gating read access to act envelopes or their edge projections violates public availability; a participant must not be confined to an arbitrary unreadable subset of the graph needed for verification. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-through-creator`` | case | Optional operating example: In the public-availability proof sketch, an absent path that would pass through the creator is indistinguishable in a gated view from one routed around the creator. The invisible path therefore prevents independent verification of that creator’s centrality. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:public-availability-around-creator`` | case | Optional operating example: In the public-availability proof sketch, an absent path routed around the creator has the same visible symptom as one passing through the creator: neither is visible. Continuous access to the full published edge set removes this ambiguity. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``subsec:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement`` | subsection | Agree on the authoritative accepted-act order and the complete supplied epoch operands, including Actor entries, prior state and debit vintage. Act order alone does not select the boundary amounts; equal complete operands reproduce equal results. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-linearization`` | case | Strict append-log linearization is a final-state agreement mechanism only if honest participants converge on the same authoritative order and complete epoch package. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-cluster-closure`` | case | Cluster-closure writes under the published closure rule are compliant only if honest participants converge on the same authoritative order and complete epoch package; ordered membership alone does not choose the accepted marker view. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-bft-agreement`` | case | A BFT agreement gadget is one way to select the ordered frontier under the declared agreement model. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-gossip-stability`` | case | Gossip replication with stability depth is compliant only if honest participants converge on the same authoritative order and complete epoch package. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-disagreement`` | case | Disagreement between participants on the authoritative order fails final epoch state agreement; formation and order-sensitive raw quantities require that common order. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``case:deployment:final-epoch-state-agreement-inconsistent-projection`` | case | A projection, envelope or admission package not deterministically derived from the agreed consumed epoch operands fails agreement. Mixing Actor entry sets from different boundaries, prior states or debit vintages, or recomputing a source history inside the closure calculation fails the supplied-input requirements. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``subsec:deployment:immutability`` | subsection | Preserve accepted record contents while permitting changes only to removable carriage. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``case:deployment:immutability-mutable-records`` | case | Mutable published edge records, including parameter updates, retroactive corrections and soft deletion, violate immutability: a retrieved record must remain valid for later replay rather than silently invalidating cached computations. | closure | invariant check | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``post:deployment:public-object-replay`` | postulate | Determine normative identity and replay from public objects rather than hashes or digests. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 7 Sorting | ``subsec:deployment:feed-reproducibility`` | subsection | Terminal feed reproduction obligations are recorded but no feed engine is proposed in Layer 1. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 7 Sorting | ``case:deployment:feed-reproducibility-closed-form`` | case | The chosen deterministic closed-form feed formula permits clients to execute the full ranking computation without server state and avoids model-weight overhead; it must remain compatible with device-local computation. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 7 Sorting | ``case:deployment:feed-reproducibility-public-model`` | case | A model with publicly distributed, locally executable weights can satisfy feed reproducibility in principle only if the client can execute the full computation without server state. Modern-scale weight storage and inference requirements conflict with the device-local invariant described here. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 7 Sorting | ``case:deployment:feed-reproducibility-consensus-ranking`` | case | Consensus-computed ranking refreshed per epoch satisfies feed reproducibility only if a client can execute the full computation without server state. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 7 Sorting | ``case:deployment:feed-reproducibility-opaque-model`` | case | Opaque server-held model weights inaccessible to clients fail feed reproducibility: clients cannot independently compute or falsify the claimed ranking. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:device-local-depth-four`` | case | Depth four is the chosen example for an optional device-local service; its cacheability depends on the declared device and degree assumptions and is not a closure-admission condition. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:device-local-depth-three`` | case | Depth three is an alternative for an optional device-local service, assessed against the same declared cacheability assumptions. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:transparency-of-protocol-formulas-edition-fixed`` | case | An optional communication practice fixes formulas and constants within its announced edition; closure evaluates the supplied specified calculation regardless of that announcement practice. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:transparency-of-protocol-formulas-scheduled-vote`` | case | Optional operating example: Governance-vote updates are compatible with formula transparency only on a published schedule with enough lead time for clients to recompute affected cached values before the change applies.
