| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:transparency-of-protocol-formulas-time-locked`` | case | Optional operating example: Time-locked update windows with independent oracle attestation are compatible with formula transparency only if clients cannot be surprised by silent changes; the declaration remains fixed within its structural edition. Its service property is not a condition of the calculation; retain any selected local service duty separately. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``case:deployment:transparency-of-protocol-formulas-silent-tuning`` | case | Silent changes frustrate the optional transparency practice and cached readouts; the calculation still uses its supplied inputs, and a public record does not reveal private purpose. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``subsec:deployment:calibration-declarations`` | subsection | Group optional communication of selected calculation inputs; the inputs themselves remain those of the separately labelled calculation. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``post:deployment:calibration-declarations`` | postulate | Supplied standing, wall, boundary-policy, encoding and replay inputs fix the completed epoch calculation. Their communication and selection are outside it. Standing normalization, wall comparison and debit-vintage rules, bounded candidate search and fallback, exact encoding and supported descending rung attempts determine the relevant checks. | closure | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:deployment:standing-parameters`` | condition | Consume the tilt profile and finite grid, normalized nonnegative depth masses, deepest support, positive self-retention, nonnegative domain weights and normalized family profiles. Deepest positive support pays its full cumulative tilt. Profile normalization removes duplicate domain counting but leaves domain-weight scale independent; row normalization conserves the allocated unit. Partition invariance needs additional premises beyond these inputs. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:deployment:wall-calibration`` | condition | Consume the numeraire, policy-floor schedule, exact structural floor and defining object, margin, controller formula, initialization, reduced constants and arithmetic/comparison rules. Refine or reject an unresolved binding-floor proposal before acceptance. Keep proposal and delivered wall distinct through floor, deadband and step cap, and charge the writing-epoch vintage. A changed structural floor changes an input, not an epoch output. | closure | policy | pn-epoch | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:deployment:boundary-policy`` | condition | Declare size, duration bounds, floor and announcement lead, candidate/trial budget, timeout and fallback; an unwritten candidate has no admission right. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:deployment:canonical-encoding`` | condition | The supplied encoding resolves exact values, Actor and support ordering, rational representations, nonexact disclosures and the profile, depth-mass, emission, controller and certificate fields. The paper chooses no byte encoding. | closure | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``post:deployment:replay-verification`` | postulate | Decide stated integer/rational values and exact enclosure endpoints exactly; a definition-stated object uses its deciding identity. Every recorded comparison resolves its actual instance, support on both sides, threshold, reduction and procedure edition; support is supplied or uniquely recoverable. A definition-only side without a deciding identity cannot support INDECISIVE. Endpoint self-consistency and independent soundness verification are distinct, and inability establishes neither confirmation nor discrepancy. An unresolved binding set is rejected and absent from accepted publication; a rung straddle remains in its ordered accepted-set attempt record. Courtesy presentation is separate. | closure | policy | pn-replay | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 6 Epoch | ``cond:deployment:canonical-replay`` | condition | Record positive rung attempts in descending order through the first PASS or the least positive rung, each with supplied or recoverable sound support; the anchor has no row. Missing, repeated, misplaced or unsupported attempts cannot justify descent or acceptance. A reader unable to check confirms and disputes nothing; an unresolved binding comparison rejects the proposed set. Partition comparisons require their explicit fibre and history inputs, which the record does not supply as person associations or counterfactual histories. | closure | policy | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``subsec:deployment:verdict-certificates`` | subsection | Accepted-record verdicts carry their required support and evidence; rejection creates no accepted publication duty. Definitions and lemmas fix certificate self-consistency and soundness relations; optional issuance has its own exact courtesy label. | closure | policy | pn-replay | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``def:deployment:verdict-certificate`` | definition | A certificate is the triple of standard reference, evidence support and outcome for one resolved comparison instance. The standard fixes the predicate, threshold, reduction, boundary and editions; support is an exact stated value, recoverable standard enclosure, supplied dynamic enclosure or deciding exact identity. A dynamic producer choice requires supplied endpoints; recovering endpoints does not establish soundness. Outcome agrees with the resolved support, and a bare certificate asserts no acceptance. | closure | type | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``lem:deployment:stated-verdict-replay`` | lemma | Replayers resolving the same certificate support under the same standard and public record agree on its stated decision. Self-consistency alone does not establish enclosure soundness, which also needs the bound inputs. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``lem:deployment:sound-verdict-agreement`` | lemma | Sound enclosures of one comparison under one standard never decide opposite ways; their intersection is sound and decides whatever either decides, a contained sound enclosure keeps any decision, a containing one never reverses one, and where the value equals an exact threshold an enclosure decides an order test exactly when the endpoint on which the test turns is the value and equality only as the point enclosure, while an enclosure or refinement keeping the value in its interior stays INDECISIVE. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``post:deployment:verdict-issuance`` | postulate | For a realization adopting the courtesy issuance standard, present the existing computed-and-included comparison data whole to its rightful holder, binding the issuer by a trusted association. The duty promises neither a computation nor a delivery hop; a holder checks resolved support and outcome, and soundness needs the bound inputs. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``post:deployment:rejected-verdicts`` | postulate | An operator may show a rejected proposed set’s certificate outside the accepted package; notification supplies no accepted act or admission requirement. Accepted certificate membership is fixed by the host contract and replay rules. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``rem:deployment:stated-exactness`` | remark | Decide what is stated exactly however it was computed; when every stated enclosure is sound and every declared reduction is an exact equivalence, inexactness of any kind can only leave a verdict INDECISIVE, and realizations that state the same data reach the same verdicts. | closure | invariant check | pn-replay | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``subsec:deployment:centralized-monitoring`` | subsection | Group optional observation and reporting practices, not additional feedback or admission conditions. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``post:deployment:centralized-monitoring`` | postulate | An optional monitoring standard may report accepted calculations; missing a target changes neither the floor, exact standing, certificate verdict nor input quarantine. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:certificate-monitoring`` | condition | An optional monitor may display the accepted certificate, width, rung and supplied or recoverable enclosure support; the replay floor’s independent certificate obligations remain. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:emission-experience`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: The operator publishes wall, emission and exported-pair distributions, sag and rehabilitation with source rates, rung, interval and changed supports; declare thresholds, persistence, review and response. Report the export cap without a small-movement guarantee; no observation adds controller inputs or silently changes emission. Epoch bites 5 and 6 supply fields, host bite 8 publishes, and deployment owns comparisons. For declared funded partitions report actual export beside both fibre ceilings, Registration debit beside incremental burn, and maximum-standing concentration; the same concentration exposure can measure a legitimately separate role under ``ex:epoch:split-showcase``. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:controller-performance`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Publish plant/activity/burn hypotheses, tested range and tracking, error, settling and chatter including failures; include price, capacity, emission and below-wall fractions. Declare review criteria for step and deadband calibration; failures authorize neither escalation nor new inputs nor unconditional tracking claims. Epoch bite 7 supplies controller results, host bite 8 publishes, and deployment owns trials. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:standing-work`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Measure per epoch/trial edges, support, width, clearance, SCCs, passes, residuals, invalidations and elapsed time against declared workload, hardware, arithmetic and memory envelopes; report missed targets and use declared fallback without dropping relations or approximating standing. Bites 6, 7 and 11 supply scoped work evidence; deployment owns operational percentile publication. These are the work-envelope measurements for exact conserved evaluation, including measured benefit of adopted reuse. | courtesy | policy | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:cadence-maturity`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: The deployment measures raw maturity drift and terminal observations when boundary timing changes, publishes adaptation-window freeze evidence and threshold responses. Raw maturity never becomes a projected-standing input. Host bite 8 publishes boundary facts and readouts bite 10 supplies the optional raw service; terminal-consumer computation remains a deployment duty under (`open:inventory:deployment-terminal-evidence`). | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``subsec:deployment:calibration-evidence`` | subsection | Group optional comparisons of chosen operating parameters and implementations. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``post:deployment:calibration-evidence`` | postulate | A deployment may publish comparative evidence under a chosen service standard; it creates neither a calculation constant nor an independent-audit duty. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:parameter-comparisons`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Publish baseline/candidate inputs, affected derived values, criteria and regenerated evidence for structural parameter changes. Keep temperature fixed absent charter adoption; recompute tier, discovery and margin comparisons, and distinguish recoinage from structural migration without holding standing fixed. Bites 6, 7, 10 and 11 furnish relevant fixtures; deployment owns criteria, terminal comparisons and review, with no hidden controller feedback. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:payload-cache-envelope`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Publish payload cap, indexing/encoding overhead, cache budget, traversal/depth-four envelope, parallel complete-act workload and retention horizon. Count one allowance per authored act and none for derived edges; reject oversized candidate workload/cap combinations. Deployment owns capacity sizing; records validation and host bite 8 support declared bounds, while terminal cache computation stays held under (`open:inventory:deployment-terminal-evidence`). | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:boundary-search-evidence`` | condition | A selected comparison service may publish current inputs and output/reuse comparisons; exact reuse itself remains justified by the calculation’s equality conditions, not by adoption of this service. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:terminal-regression`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: For each standing-edition change, publish consumer-specific inputs, deltas, fit/tolerance and failure response using actual dependencies; insulated CAN does not thereby read standing. Readouts bite 10 and corpus bite 11 cover the supported raw surface; other terminal comparisons belong to deployments under (`open:inventory:deployment-terminal-evidence`). | courtesy | fixture | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``cond:deployment:complete-act-conformance`` | condition | Within the optional courtesy standard: Publish compiler/replay evidence for parallel authored acts, identities, folds, recipient resolution, domain profiles and allocation under original ledger/threshold vintages. Reproduce the accepted-act census; source inspection or a certificate on incomplete projections is insufficient. Bites 3 through 6 build the semantics, replay bite 9 reconstructs them and bite 11 composes the evidence. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| Appendix I — Deployment | ``subsec:deployment:concealment-entropy`` | subsection | Within the optional courtesy standard: Assign entropy custody for the author’s content blinding value to the Actor or carriage layer under a published generation rule, separately from the host’s blinding values. The carrier checks format, length, freshness and non-reuse and removes both pair members atomically. These are carriage duties, never act-acceptance conditions. Published P contains no host blinding value; content concealment depends on the author’s content blinding value even for a holder of the disclosed dependency blinding value. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 04 Graph | ``cond:graph:ordering-accountability-parent-placement`` | condition | A parent-placement proof shows that the host completed an act violating its public parents. | 03 Network | ``def:network:material-classification`` | definition | Every exact label defaults to closure unless expressly oracle or courtesy; mathematics is closure material. Closure checks supplied operands, including adjacent epochs and carried state, without evaluating an oracle warrant. Oracle replay projects public second-party data and rules; courtesy standards are optional and their failure does not invalidate an epoch. Child labels classify independently, unlabeled text inherits its enclosing owner, and neither closure nor oracle depends on courtesy. | closure | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:projection`` | definition | A projection is determined by its stated source and rule; materializing it is a cache with no independent authority. | closure | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

| 03 Network | ``post:deployment:replay-floor-scope`` | postulate | The replay floor contains calculation and recorded-signature evidence, not separate source or authority oracle histories, declarations or their editions. | 02 Comparator | ``post:comparator:public-provenance-replay`` | postulate | Replay provenance from the relevant public rules and history; unavailable history establishes no positive oracle result and no calculation discrepancy. | oracle | policy | pn-host | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``def:comparator:burn-history-inputs`` | definition | Identify the public rules and history that the provenance oracle reads; no pinned declaration or source-proof membership in the epoch follows. | oracle | type | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:author-authority-oracle`` | definition | The authority oracle judges whether recorded signing keys speak for the stated Actor from the applicable public authority history, separately from recorded-signature validity. | oracle | type | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:authority-history-inputs`` | definition | Supply the authority history and rules needed to assess the recorded keys and Actor; the epoch itself has no mandatory source-warrantor or declaration-edition field. | oracle | type | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``post:graph:public-author-authority-replay`` | postulate | A changed or unavailable authority history changes the oracle result without reassigning the recorded author, debit, standing, title or settlement consequences. | oracle | policy | pn-graph | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:deployment:author-authority-validity-projection`` | definition | Assess an accepted act's signing keys against the applicable public authority history. | 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:burn-realization-audit-evidence`` | remark | Public source history can be inspected for provenance and external price. | 04 Graph | ``def:graph:courtesy-handshake`` | definition | The reference wire construction uses canonical serialization, separated hashes, signatures and independent author and host blinding values for proposal, commitments, seal and exact approval. Its signing notation identifies a key, not an Actor or authority proof. Optional content-pair carriage has separate checks and receipts; dependency fraud proofs open the complete list against its commitment. Wire choices and entropy formats are courtesy matters, distinct from the structural closure contract. | courtesy | policy | pn-act-authentication | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:requested-publication-intent`` | definition | Optional peek, store, serve, shelve and forget requests carry wishes, never opening existence. A signed initial request is the origin of the sequence for its act, projection and party, and its receipt binds that origin; later request sequences exceed it. An identical retry in the receipt-bound values returns the one receipt already issued. Legacy bodies imply no intent under this edition. | courtesy | policy | pn-act-profile | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:dependency-list-order`` | definition | The reference list orders author bytes, numeric local sequence and family bytes, strictly increasing without duplicates; this represents a finite set and adds no graph ordering rule. | courtesy | serialization | pn-act-profile | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:dependency-nonce-concealment`` | remark | Concealment after removal depends on the author’s undisclosed blinding value and independent values elsewhere; copies on the submission path cannot be recalled, and the warning imposes no quality floor. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``gloss:graph:commitment`` | remark | Commitment means binding and concealing under the declared cryptographic premise. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``gloss:graph:opening`` | remark | An opening supplies the complete content and blinding values needed to check its commitment; it supplies no selective disclosure. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``gloss:graph:blinding-value`` | remark | A blinding value is a secret meant to carry high entropy, separate by author/host and projection; disclosure follows its opening boundary and does not establish its quality. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``gloss:graph:peek`` | remark | Peek means examine without taking. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:holder`` | definition | Holding is possession of opening material, not a service undertaking or graph state; transient sight is not holding. | courtesy | none | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:storage`` | definition | Storage quietly retains opening material without an endpoint or serving duty until the custodian removes it. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:custodian`` | definition | A holder becomes custodian by undertaking storage or carriage, not by receipt alone; its promise binds itself and ends when its controlled material is removed. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``def:graph:carriage`` | definition | Carriage adds serving at an authenticated endpoint to storage; acceptance and transitions are acknowledged separately from append and never determine act validity. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:wall-calibration-oversight`` | remark | An adopter evaluates both chartered floors against declared economic and work criteria; a third floor needs its own derivation. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:boundary-policy-oversight`` | remark | An adopter publishes the availability target and constant-change adaptation window and freezes cadence retargeting during it; these are conduct observations, not epoch inputs. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:canonical-encoding-oversight`` | remark | An adopter supplies canonical round-trip evidence for the published encoding; it is implementation evidence, not an extra epoch input. | courtesy | fixture | pn-verification | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:centralized-monitoring-conduct`` | remark | An adopter publishes reproducible observations, acquisition procedures, thresholds, persistence windows, review schedule and crossing dispositions. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:title-certificate-monitoring-courtesies`` | remark | An adopter publishes title-fold disagreements and their evidence and triggers its published review without rewriting the certificate. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:certificate-monitoring-courtesies`` | remark | An adopter publishes movement, hop mass, below-wall fractions and Registration/vouch cases, fallback and verdict frequencies, thresholds, reviews and scoped partition comparisons; none is a new replay dependency. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:calibration-publication-conduct`` | remark | An adopter publishes calibration inputs or acquisition, editions, method, predeclared criteria, results and limitations and records failure without replacing the comparison. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| Appendix I — Deployment | ``rem:deployment:boundary-search-courtesies`` | remark | An adopter publishes trials, elapsed envelopes, failures and headroom, hardware and measured reuse benefit without claiming optimal selection or unseen-demand completeness; performance reports do not replace accepted-output evidence. | courtesy | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-exogeneity`` | condition | Graph topology and graph operations do not supply the exogenous frame gross amount. ``cond:comparator:burn-exogenous-origin``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-instantaneous-cost`` | condition | Obtaining the attributed amount consumes a resource priced at the instant of its source event. ``cond:comparator:burn-costly-append-only-record``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-irrecoverability`` | condition | The source event cannot be reversed to recover what it consumed. ``cond:comparator:burn-costly-append-only-record``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-monotone-history`` | condition | Successive frame gross amounts are nondecreasing under the stated connection premises; this condition does not concern balances after debits. ``cond:comparator:burn-pinned-monotonicity`` and ``cond:comparator:burn-costly-append-only-record``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-neutrality`` | condition | Frame gross amounts use one common magnitude unit across actors. ``cond:comparator:burn-neutrality``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-non-negativity`` | condition | Frame gross amounts inhabit a nonnegative ordered magnitude domain. ``cond:comparator:burn-non-negativity``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-non-repayment`` | condition | The supplied input confers no redemption or repayment entitlement. ``cond:comparator:burn-neutrality``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-opaque-identity-link`` | condition | The attributed frame amount links the opaque actor identity to what it denotes in the source realization, without defining an address ontology or signing authority. ``cond:comparator:burn-bindability``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-source-backing`` | condition | An attributed amount is backed by a genuine irrecoverable source event and does not exceed that event's attributable magnitude. ``cond:comparator:burn-provenance-auditability``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-source-persistence`` | condition | An attributed contribution does not move to another identity or duplicate through its representation; alternative funded partitions may still change standing. ``cond:comparator:burn-non-transferability``. | closure | policy | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:burn-finalization-pin`` | condition | Pin the source amount at an identified finalization boundary. | oracle | invariant check | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:burn-no-privileged-accrual`` | condition | Operating a realization supplies no privilege to accrue source amount outside its costly source rule; this is an oracle premise, not a numerical operator cap. | oracle | invariant check | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``cond:comparator:finalized-burn-amount`` | condition | The source primitive supplies finalization boundaries and their fixed attributed amounts. | oracle | invariant check | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``def:comparator:actor-entry`` | definition | An Actor entry supplies an opaque Actor identifier and its frame gross amount at a boundary; the type does not certify source backing or external cost. | closure | type | pn-records | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``def:comparator:burn-projection`` | definition | Separate source amounts, price and source interpretation from the Actor-entry operands consumed by closure. | oracle | type | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``post:comparator:actor-entry-assumptions`` | postulate | State the thirteen Actor-entry hypotheses separately from the oracle claims that can discharge their source interpretation; the closure calculates over supplied values. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:closing-survey`` | remark | Finalization of a public source history can support a stable supplied snapshot; its timing and external economic effects are realization matters (``post:comparator:marker-bound-finalization``). Internally recognized title does not establish the truth of an asserted external event (``rem:graph:settlement-reputation``, ``rem:graph:item-identity-limit``). This is the interpretive limit of ``cond:comparator:source-edge-event``; reputation can price a false claim through ``post:comparator:accountability-cost``. The no-privileged-accrual assumption is explicit at ``cond:comparator:actor-entry-no-privileged-accrual``; the calculation neither infers it from an operating role nor evaluates a realization's source distribution. | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:external-outlay-projection`` | remark | Multiply each stated frame-amount benchmark by the source realization’s published unit cost to obtain the corresponding proportional outlay in its external denomination; this is an oracle projection, not another calculation operand. | oracle | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:external-value-necessity`` | remark | Costliness of the source action requires value supplied outside closure; the oracle attaches to that action, while the calculation assumes exogeneity and does not evaluate source history. | oracle | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:numeraire-fiat-choice`` | remark | When the positive numeraire value is supplied by fiat, its choice and standing are matters of reputation. The network consumes the value without a record field naming a warrantor, and the oracle does not choose or set it. | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:pre-opening-capacity-warning`` | remark | A pre-opening source interval can require an economic warning; the realization owes no published aggregate capacity bound, and an absent interval supplies no bound duty. | oracle | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:sincere-audit-benefit`` | remark | Passing sincere audits can earn good reputation. Sincerity and the significance of reputation are persuasive judgments. The specification imposes no duty to conduct an audit, obtain a verdict, publish audit evidence, or meet a reputation assessment. | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:source-backing-discharge`` | remark | The oracle can compare the supplied amount with publicly available realization history through a deterministic provenance projection. This check concerns the source of the amount and is not an operand of the frame arithmetic. | oracle | policy | pn-host | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:source-faithfulness-interpretation`` | remark | Source provenance is distinct from the truth of a claim carried by an accepted act. Costliness and no privileged source accrual are assumed for the supplied amount; the oracle can examine public source history through ``rem:comparator:source-backing-discharge``. Neither the amount nor a valid internal title fold establishes external event truth. Equal public records cannot distinguish legitimate separation from strategic concentration (``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``). | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``rem:comparator:source-limit-survey`` | remark | The frame amount does not state what obtaining it cost its actor net of external holdings. The conditional external outlay of ``eq:comparator:admission-benchmark-deposits`` does not determine that net cost. A realization's own rules can limit an initial interval (``rem:comparator:pre-opening-capacity-warning``); this grants no later costless-source guarantee. Remedies for strategic concentration apply equally to a legitimate completion with the same public fields (``thm:epoch:split-observational-equivalence``). | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 02 Comparator | ``subsec:comparator:actor-entry-assumptions`` | subsection | Track actor entry assumptions as a separately addressable subsection. | closure | none | none | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``post:deployment:specified-calculation-inputs`` | postulate | Supply the specified Actor entries, acts, carried state, free constants and specification version; the calculation adds no procedure for identifying a source warrantor. | closure | policy | pn-records | False | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``rem:network:non-adoption-declaration-courtesy`` | remark | Declaring non-adoption of an optional practice is itself courtesy, not an added calculation or admission duty. | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 03 Network | ``rem:network:optional-host-conduct`` | remark | Optional publication, notification, reproduction and service conduct adds no closure input or admission requirement. | courtesy | policy | none | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |
| 04 Graph | ``rem:graph:settlement-coordinate-conjunction`` | remark | The positive commercial stance conjunction reads the three p_d marginals, not bilinear parity; title is sentiment-independent and counterparty standing allocation still requires the complete recognized, unchallenged, title-transferring fold. | closure | policy | pn-graph | True | network-v0.37.0-draft.1; explicit |

## Constants · `sec:inventory:constants`

**Observation (Source definitions)** · `obs:inventory:constants` Empty class and citation fields preserve absence in the released collection. The dataset retains source literal strings, expressions, function selectors and display rules in its definition field; a function selector is not an evaluated numeric result or a certificate. It contains 2830 declarations and the separate standing_profile, verification_fixtures and act_time_fixture records. The verification profile retains the published exact inputs, precision and comparison settings for the released finite witnesses; their scope is the declared fixtures, not a universal performance or economic claim. The standing profile preserves exact rational strings for hop shape, depth masses, self-retention and certified strength, plus the domain-weight mode, grounding the reference case in ``ver:standing:conserved-equilibrium-enclosure``. The act-time fixture retains ordered acts, epochs, incidence, public parents, opened dependencies and published stamps for ``ver:boundary:act-time-recomputation``. Its result selectors name exact integer replay results; they are not substituted evaluated values. The input schema is peer-constants-2; the added profile does not imply that old readers understand its additional member. Generated decimal displays alone supply no numerical proof.

| Recorded class | Proposed home | Entries |

**Model (Reference standing profile)** · `model:inventory:standing-profile` The exact hop shape is 1, 1/2, 1/4, 1/8, the normalized depth masses are 1/2, 1/4, 1/8, 1/8, self-retention is 1, domain weighting is uniform_unit, and certified strength is 1. No intermediate rung grid is inferred from this reference declaration; deployment grids remain separate.

**Open Question (Bridge reaction placement)** · `open:inventory:bridge-reaction-placement` Owner: the deploying operator for the published centralized-phase reaction, and the repository architecture reviewer, the role that reviews scope and crate-responsibility changes for repository acceptance, for resolving the implementation boundary. ``case:transport:bridge-eligibility-cliff`` requires the threshold, share-volatility companion and conditional reference-rule publication; ``rem:bridge:eligibility-cliff-primary`` makes flip frequency a distribution-stability measure. The existing responsibility map gives pn-readouts published standing/title and optional raw views, and pn-host package publication, while placing bridge computation, guild policy and CAN outside the implementation (`conv:design:scope`), (`rec:decomposition:decision`). It does not assign computation of bridge selection and normalized-share history, so neither crate is inferred as owner. Settle the question either through a deployment-owned computation over published epoch certificates, recording its metric and publication contract, or through an accepted scope amendment assigning that computation and contract to a crate; until then these rows retain kind policy and no crate. The publication obligation is required even though adopting its terminal reference rule remains optional.

**Open Question (Deployment terminal evidence placement)** · `open:inventory:deployment-terminal-evidence` Owner: the deploying operator for terminal consumers and their published measurements, and the repository architecture reviewer, the role reviewing scope and crate-responsibility changes, for any implementation assignment. The optional cadence/maturity, payload/cache and terminal-regression scopes of ``cond:deployment:cadence-maturity``, ``cond:deployment:payload-cache-envelope`` and ``cond:deployment:terminal-regression`` include CAN, bridge or application measurements outside (`conv:design:scope`). Existing bites supply canonical epoch certificates, optional raw service and conformance inputs, but do not assign those consumers or cache deployments to a crate. No monitoring standard is adopted. Settle any future undertaking by a deployment-owned measurement contract over published outputs, or by an accepted scope amendment assigning its implementation; these inventory rows assign no new crate or bite. Local finite comparisons remain usable without a courtesy-standard claim. realizes{``cond:deployment:cadence-maturity``; This scope hold names unassigned publication and comparison duties without asserting measured terminal performance.} realizes{``cond:deployment:payload-cache-envelope``; This scope hold names unassigned publication and comparison duties without asserting measured terminal performance.} realizes{``cond:deployment:terminal-regression``; This scope hold names unassigned publication and comparison duties without asserting measured terminal performance.}

**Convention (Deployment inventory scope)** · `conv:inventory:deployment-scope` The deployment rows retain the paper's statements without asserting their external premises. Their repository conformance classification and declared conditional dependencies are the generated tables of (`rule:testing:public-record-conformance`); proposed homes and citation status supply no witness of hardware, availability, elapsed time or private search effort.
